== Torneo Apertura de la LPF de AFA 2025  ‹§1apertura›

[2025 AFA LPF Opening Tournament]
Non official table
Tie break:
1. Points won.
2. Goal difference.
3. Goals for.
4. Head-to-head.
5. Fair play 1: Yellow card (1), Second yellow card (3), Straight red card (5).
6. Fair play 2: Quantity of red cards.
7. Lot.

== Copa País de Selecciones de Liga 2025 - 2025 CFFA Copa País Regional League Combineds  ‹§copapais›

Non official and full table (All stages and all regions)
Ordered by round reached, points, position in the group, goal difference, goals for, goals away.
Round reached
  #.Full name                                           MP   W  D  L  Gf- Ga   P (Headquarters on)
<!-- $table73$ - Champion -->

Tie-breaks used in groups of three or four teams:
 1 Head-to-head points.
 2 Head-to-head goal difference.
 3 Head-to-head goals for.
 4 Head-to-head goals for as away.
 5 Repeat 1-4 if is necessary.
 6 Goal difference in the group.
 7 Goals for in the group.
 8 Goals for as away in the group.
 9 Repeat 6-8 if is necessary.
10 Lot.

Tie-breaks used in elimination rounds:
 1 Goal difference.
 2 Penalty Shot Out (PSO).
Tie-break used in Final Stage:
1 Penalty Shot Out (PSO).
